
-- proto 22
TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

applyVexonStyle = function(arg, arg2)
	-- proto t1442
	arg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
	Instance.new("UICorner", arg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
	local uiStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", arg)
	uiStroke.Thickness = 1.5
	uiStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(170, 0, 255)
	uiStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
	local colorSequence = ColorSequence.new

	Instance.new("UIGradient", uiStroke).Color = colorSequence({
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255)),
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(150, 0, 255)),
	})

	if arg2 then
		uiStroke.Transparency = 0.1
	else
		uiStroke.Transparency = 0.4
	end
end

checkExecutorSupport = function()
	-- proto 25
	if _G.VexonHub and _G.VexonHub.SystemInfo then
	end

	local executorScore = _G.VexonHub and _G.VexonHub.ExecutorScore or { unc = 0, sunc = 0, total = 0, supported = false }
	totalSupport = executorScore.total
	isSupported = executorScore.supported
	unc_support = executorScore.unc
	sunc_support = executorScore.sunc
	return { supported = isSupported, unc = unc_support, sunc = sunc_support }
end

					lib:Notify({
						Title = "Tabs Loaded",

		-- proto 28
		createCirclePart = function(arg, arg2, arg3, transparency)
			-- proto 29
			local part = Instance.new("Part")
			part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
			part.Size = Vector3.new(arg2, arg * 2, arg * 2)
			part.CFrame = CFrame.new(arg3) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 1.5707963267948966)
			part.Anchored = true
			part.Transparency = transparency
			part.CanCollide = true
			part.CastShadow = false
			part.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
			part.Parent = game.Workspace
			return part
		end

		createCirclePart(100000, 0.5, Vector3.new(0, -500, 0), 0.5)
		createCirclePart(150, 0.5, Vector3.new(-68, 0, 20354), 0.5)
		createCirclePart(25, 1, Vector3.new(150, 500, 30), 0.5)

				end

			local function fn7()
				-- proto t163
				if connection_2 then
					connection_2:Disconnect()
					connection_2 = nil
				end

				if not flag3 then
					return
				end

				connection_2 = RunService_.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					-- proto t165
					if not flag3 then
						return
					end

					if not v3 or not v or not v.Parent then
						return
					end
					local position = v3.Position

					for _, player_ in ipairs(Players_:GetPlayers()) do
						local character_ = player_.Character
						local humanoidRootPart_ = character_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local humanoid_ = character_:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

						if not (not humanoidRootPart_ or not humanoid_) then
						end

						local animator2 = humanoid_:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

						local ok, result = pcall(function()
							-- proto t169
							return animator2:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
						end)

						local flag4 = false

						for _, v4 in ipairs(result) do
							local match = tostring(v4.Animation and v4.Animation.AnimationId or ""):match("%d+")
							if match and tbl5[match] then
								flag4 = true
								break
							end
						end

						if flag4 or character_:FindFirstChild("M1ing") then
							local n6 = position - humanoidRootPart_.Position
							local n7 = (n6.Magnitude > 1e-06 and n6.Unit or v3.CFrame.RightVector) * n5

							pcall(function()
								-- proto t168
								v3.CFrame = v3.CFrame + n7
							end)

							break
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			Stopallanimation = function()

			playAnimation = function(arg)
				-- proto t354
				local v, v2 = getCharacter()
				if not v2 then
					return
				end
				local animator = v2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

				if not animator then
					animator = Instance.new("Animator")
					animator.Parent = v2
				end

				local animation = animCache[arg]

				if not animation then
					animation = Instance.new("Animation")
					animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(arg)
					animCache[arg] = animation
				end

				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					-- proto t358
					return animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
				end)

				if not ok or not result then
					return
				end
				result.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
				result:Play()

				pcall(function()
					-- proto t355
					result:AdjustSpeed(1.2)
				end)

				return result
			end

			rotateCharacter = function(arg)
				-- proto t363
				if isPerformingAction then
					return
				end
				isPerformingAction = true
				local v = getCharacter()
				local humanoidRootPart_ = v and v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if humanoidRootPart_ then
					pcall(function()
						-- proto t365
						humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(arg), 0)
						camPos = camera and camera.CFrame.Position or humanoidRootPart_.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
						camY = camPos.Y
						distanceVec = camPos - humanoidRootPart_.Position
						flatVec = Vector3.new(distanceVec.X, 0, distanceVec.Z)
						local v2 = flatVec
						rotatedVec = CFrame.fromAxisAngle(Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), math.rad(arg)) * v2
						newCamPos = humanoidRootPart_.Position + rotatedVec

						if camera and camera:IsA("Camera") then
							local position = humanoidRootPart_.Position
							camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(newCamPos.X, camY, newCamPos.Z), position)
						end
					end)
				end

				isPerformingAction = false
			end

			sideDash = function(arg, arg2, arg3)
				-- proto t366
				if isPerformingAction then
					return
				end
				isPerformingAction = true
				local humanoidRootPart_ = getCharacter()
				humanoidRootPart_ = humanoidRootPart_ and humanoidRootPart_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if humanoidRootPart_ then
					local tween_ = TweenService:Create(humanoidRootPart_, TweenInfo.new(arg3, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { CFrame = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame * CFrame.new(arg * arg2, 0, 0) })

					pcall(function()
						-- proto t369
						tween_:Play()
					end)

					if tween_ then
						pcall(function()
							-- proto t368
							tween_.Completed:Wait()
						end)
					end

					pcall(function()
						-- proto t367
						tween_:Destroy()
					end)
				end

				isPerformingAction = false
			end

			impulseDash = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
				-- proto t370
				if isPerformingAction then
					return
				end
				isPerformingAction = true
				local humanoidRootPart_ = getCharacter()
				humanoidRootPart_ = humanoidRootPart_ and humanoidRootPart_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if humanoidRootPart_ then
					local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
					local n = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame.RightVector * arg * arg2 + Vector3.new(0, arg3, 0)

					if n.Magnitude == 0 then
						n = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
					end

					bodyVelocity.Velocity = n.Unit * arg4
					bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
					bodyVelocity.Parent = humanoidRootPart_

					task.delay(arg5, function()
						-- proto t371
						pcall(function()
							-- proto t372
							bodyVelocity:Destroy()
						end)
					end)
				end

				isPerformingAction = false
			end

			doM1Reset = function()
				-- proto t374
				if not TechStates.M1Enabled then
					return
				end
				Stopallanimation()

				pcall(function()
					-- proto t378
					playAnimation(10480793962)
				end)

				pcall(function()
					-- proto t375
					sideDash(1, 26, 0.22)
				end)

				pcall(function()
					-- proto t376
					rotateCharacter(70)
				end)

				task.wait(0.003)

				pcall(function()
					-- proto t377
					if VIM and type(VIM.SendKeyEvent) == "function" then
						VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
						VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
					end
				end)
			end

			doEmoteDash = function()
				-- proto t359
				if not TechStates.EmoteEnabled then
					return
				end
				Stopallanimation()

				pcall(function()
					-- proto t362
					playAnimation(10480793962)
				end)

				pcall(function()
					-- proto t360
					rotateCharacter(90)
				end)

				pcall(function()
					-- proto t361
					impulseDash(1, 38, 8, 95, 0.27)
				end)
			end

			M1Reset = Tabs.Tech:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "M1 Reset Macro", Icon = "box" })
			m1MobileBtnInstance = nil

			M1Reset:Toggle({
				Title = "M1 Reset",
				Flag = "tsbm1resettoggle",
				Desc = "Toggle Of All M1 Reset",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(m1Enabled)
					-- proto t11024
					TechStates.M1Enabled = m1Enabled
				end,
			})

			M1Reset:Toggle({
				Title = "M1 Reset Mobile UI",
				Flag = "tsbm1resetmobileui",
				Desc = "Opens Mobile UI to Easy to use M1 Reset",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t343
					if arg then
						if not m1MobileBtnInstance then
							m1MobileBtnInstance = createDraggableButton("M1Btn", "M1 Reset", UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 200), function()
								-- proto t344
								pcall(doM1Reset)
							end)
						end
					elseif m1MobileBtnInstance then
						m1MobileBtnInstance:Destroy()
						m1MobileBtnInstance = nil
					end
				end,
			})

			M1Reset:Keybind({
				Title = "Keybind",
				Flag = "tsbm1resetkeybind",
				Desc = "Keybind to Use M1 Reset Tech",
				Value = "",
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t345
					pcall(function()
						-- proto t347
						TechStates.M1Key = Enum.KeyCode[arg]
					end)
				end,
			})

			EmoteDash = Tabs.Tech:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Emote Dash Macro", Icon = "box" })
			emoteMobileBtnInstance = nil

			EmoteDash:Toggle({
				Title = "Emote Dash",
				Flag = "tsbemotedashtoggle",
				Desc = "Toggle Of All Emote Dash",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(emoteEnabled)
					-- proto t348
					TechStates.EmoteEnabled = emoteEnabled
				end,
			})

			EmoteDash:Toggle({
				Title = "Emote Dash Mobile UI",
				Flag = "tsbemotedashuitoggle",
				Desc = "Opens Mobile UI to Easy to use Emote Dash",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t341
					if arg then
						if not emoteMobileBtnInstance then
							emoteMobileBtnInstance = createDraggableButton("EmoteBtn", "Emote Dash", UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 260), function()
								-- proto t342
								pcall(doEmoteDash)
							end)
						end
					elseif emoteMobileBtnInstance then
						emoteMobileBtnInstance:Destroy()
						emoteMobileBtnInstance = nil
					end
				end,
			})

			EmoteDash:Keybind({
				Title = "Keybind",
				Flag = "tsbemotedashkeybind",
				Desc = "Keybind to Use Emote Dash Tech",
				Value = "",
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t338
					pcall(function()
						-- proto t340
						TechStates.EmoteKey = Enum.KeyCode[arg]
					end)
				end,
			})

			UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
				-- proto t337
				if gameProcessed or UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then
					return
				end

				if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
					return
				end

				if input.KeyCode == TechStates.M1Key then
					pcall(doM1Reset)
				elseif input.KeyCode == TechStates.EmoteKey then
					pcall(doEmoteDash)
				end
			end)

			TwistedAnimIds = {
				"rbxassetid://15162694192",
				"rbxassetid://14136436157",
				"rbxassetid://16552234590",
				"rbxassetid://13295936866",
				"rbxassetid://13378708199",
				"rbxassetid://10469643643",
				"rbxassetid://17889290569",
				"rbxassetid://17325537719",
				"rbxassetid://13294471966",
			}

			TwistedEnabled = false
			TwistedMode = "Normal"
			PingOffset = 0
			LethalTime = 1.65
			extraAnimId = "rbxassetid://12273188754"
			extraAnimToggleState = false
			twistedConnections = {}

			isInTable = function(arg, arg2)
				-- proto t336
				for _, v in ipairs(arg) do
					if v == arg2 then
						return true
					end
				end

				return false
			end

			task.wait(0.5)

			addExtraAnim = function()
				-- proto t333
				if not isInTable(TwistedAnimIds, extraAnimId) then
					table.insert(TwistedAnimIds, extraAnimId)
				end
			end

			removeExtraAnim = function()
				-- proto t334
				for i, v in ipairs(TwistedAnimIds) do
					if v == extraAnimId then
						table.remove(TwistedAnimIds, i)
						break
					end
				end
			end

			DisconnectTwisted = function()
				-- proto t335
				for _, v in ipairs(twistedConnections) do
					v:Disconnect()
				end

				twistedConnections = {}
			end

			MoveForward = function()
				-- proto t327
				local humanoidRootPart_ = LocalPlayerGame.Character and LocalPlayerGame.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if humanoidRootPart_ then
					humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
				end
			end

			SendDash = function()
				-- proto t328
				local tbl = { { Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q, Goal = "KeyPress" } }
				local character_ = LocalPlayerGame.Character

				if character_ and character_:FindFirstChild("Communicate") then
					character_.Communicate:FireServer(unpack(tbl))
				end
			end

			SetupNormalTwisted = function()
				-- proto t329
				local connection_ = (LocalPlayerGame.Character or LocalPlayerGame.CharacterAdded:Wait()):WaitForChild("Humanoid").AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(arg)
					-- proto t330
					if arg.Animation and isInTable(TwistedAnimIds, arg.Animation.AnimationId) and TwistedEnabled then
						local n = 0.23 + PingOffset

						if arg.Animation.AnimationId == extraAnimId then
							n = LethalTime + PingOffset
						end

						task.delay(n, function()
							-- proto t331
							MoveForward()
							SendDash()
						end)
					end
				end)

				table.insert(twistedConnections, connection_)
			end

			PerformInstantTwisted = function()
				-- proto t332
				args = { { Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q, Goal = "KeyPress" } }
				char = LocalPlayerGame.Character

				if char and char:FindFirstChild("Communicate") then
					char.Communicate:FireServer(unpack(args))
				end

				Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
				currentCFrame = Camera.CFrame
				rotatedCFrame = currentCFrame * CFrame.Angles(40, -1.5707963267948966, 180)
				Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
				local tbl = { CFrame = rotatedCFrame }
				local tween_ = game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(Camera, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), tbl)
				tween_:Play()
				tween_.Completed:Wait()
				wait(1e-05)
				local tbl2 = { CFrame = currentCFrame }
				local tween_2 = game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(Camera, TweenInfo.new(0.001, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), tbl2)
				tween_2:Play()
				tween_2.Completed:Wait()
				wait(0.1)
				local tween_3 = game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(Camera, TweenInfo.new(0.0001, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), { CFrame = currentCFrame * CFrame.Angles(-0.10471975511965978, 0.3490658503988659, 0) })
				tween_3:Play()
				tween_3.Completed:Wait()
				Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			end

			SetupInstantTwisted = function()
				-- proto t389
				local humanoid_ = (LocalPlayerGame.Character or LocalPlayerGame.CharacterAdded:Wait()):WaitForChild("Humanoid")

				local connection_ = (humanoid_:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or humanoid_.ChildAdded:Wait(function(arg)
					-- proto t392
					return arg:IsA("Animator")
				end)).AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(arg)
					-- proto t390
					if arg.Animation and isInTable(TwistedAnimIds, arg.Animation.AnimationId) and TwistedEnabled then
						local humanoidRootPart_ = LocalPlayerGame.Character and LocalPlayerGame.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if humanoidRootPart_ then
							humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame + humanoidRootPart_.CFrame.LookVector * -1 * 1
						end

						local n = 0.25 + PingOffset

						if arg.Animation.AnimationId == extraAnimId then
							n = LethalTime + PingOffset
						end

						task.delay(n, function()
							-- proto t391
							PerformInstantTwisted()
						end)
					end
				end)

				table.insert(twistedConnections, connection_)
			end

			UpdateTwistedTech = function()
				-- proto t393
				DisconnectTwisted()
				if not TwistedEnabled then
					return
				end

				if TwistedMode == "Normal" then
					SetupNormalTwisted()
				elseif TwistedMode == "Instant" then
					SetupInstantTwisted()
				end

				charConn = LocalPlayerGame.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
					-- proto t394
					task.wait(1)
					UpdateTwistedTech()
				end)

				table.insert(twistedConnections, charConn)
			end

			twistedSection = Tabs.Tech:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Twisted Tech", Icon = "box" })

			twistedSection:Toggle({
				Title = "Twisted Tech",
				Flag = "tsbtwistedtoggle",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t396
					TwistedEnabled = arg
					UpdateTwistedTech()
				end,
			})

			twistedSection:Dropdown({
				Title = "Mode",
				Flag = "tsbtwistedmode",
				Desc = "Choose between Normal and Instant",
				Values = { "Normal", "Instant" },
				Value = "Normal",
				Multi = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t395
					TwistedMode = arg

					if TwistedEnabled then
						UpdateTwistedTech()
					end
				end,
			})

			twistedSection:Slider({
				Title = "Ping Compensation",
				Flag = "tsbtwistedping",
				Desc = "Additional delay in seconds to account for lag",
				Value = { Min = 0, Max = 0.5, Default = 0 },
				Step = 0.1,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t326
					PingOffset = arg
				end,
			})

			twistedSection:Toggle({
				Title = "Garou Lethal Whirlwind Twisted",
				Flag = "tsbtwistedgaroutoggle",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t325
					extraAnimToggleState = arg

					if arg then
						addExtraAnim()
					else
						removeExtraAnim()
					end
				end,
			})

			twistedSection:Slider({
				Title = "Lethal Whirlwind Delay",
				Flag = "tsblethaldelayslider",
				Desc = "Additional delay in seconds to account for Lethal Whirlwind Twisted",
				Value = { Min = 1, Max = 2, Default = 1.65 },
				Step = 0.01,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t397
					LethalTime = arg
				end,
			})

			LocalPlayerGame = game.Players.LocalPlayer
			SupaEnabled = false
			SelectedAbilities = { "Stream", "Uppercut" }
			IsExecuting = false
			AnimConnection = nil
			CharConnection = nil
			DashDelay = 0.3
			TurnDelay = 0.26
			StreamPreDelay = 2
			AbilityIds = { Stream = "rbxassetid://12296113986", Uppercut = "rbxassetid://10503381238" }

			DoJump = function()
				-- proto t405
				local humanoid_ = LocalPlayerGame.Character and LocalPlayerGame.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

				if humanoid_ then
					humanoid_.Jump = true
				end
			end

			DoStopJump = function()
				-- proto t406
				local humanoid_ = LocalPlayerGame.Character and LocalPlayerGame.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

				if humanoid_ then
					humanoid_.Jump = false
				end
			end

			DoTurn = function()
				-- proto t398
				local humanoidRootPart_ = LocalPlayerGame.Character and LocalPlayerGame.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if humanoidRootPart_ then
					local cFrame = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame
					humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(cFrame.Position, cFrame.Position + -cFrame.LookVector)
					Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
					Camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Camera.CFrame.Position, Camera.CFrame.Position + -cFrame.LookVector)
				end
			end

			DoDash = function()
				-- proto t399
				local communicate_ = LocalPlayerGame.Character and LocalPlayerGame.Character:FindFirstChild("Communicate")

				if communicate_ then
					communicate_:FireServer({ Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q, Goal = "KeyPress" })
				end
			end

			RunSupa = function()
				-- proto t400
				DoJump()

				task.delay(DashDelay, function()
					-- proto t401
					DoDash()

					task.delay(TurnDelay, function()
						-- proto t402
						DoTurn()
						DoStopJump()
						IsExecuting = false
					end)
				end)
			end

			ExecuteSupa = function(arg)
				-- proto t403
				if IsExecuting then
					return
				end
				IsExecuting = true

				if arg == "Stream" then
					task.delay(StreamPreDelay, function()
						-- proto t404
						RunSupa()
					end)
				else
					RunSupa()
				end
			end

			SetupAnimationListener = function()
				-- proto t321
				if AnimConnection then
					AnimConnection:Disconnect()
					AnimConnection = nil
				end

				if not SupaEnabled then
					return
				end
				local humanoid_ = LocalPlayerGame.Character and LocalPlayerGame.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				if not humanoid_ then
					return
				end

				AnimConnection = humanoid_.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(arg)
					-- proto t322
					if not arg.Animation or IsExecuting then
						return
					end
					local animationId = arg.Animation.AnimationId

					for _, v in ipairs(SelectedAbilities) do
						if AbilityIds[v] == animationId then
							ExecuteSupa(v)
							break
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			OnCharacterAdded = function()
				-- proto t323
				LocalPlayerGame.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
				SetupAnimationListener()
			end

			SupaTech = Tabs.Tech:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Supa Tech", Icon = "box" })

			SupaTech:Toggle({
				Title = "Enable Auto Supa",
				Flag = "tsbautosupatoggle",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t320
					SupaEnabled = arg

					if arg then
						SetupAnimationListener()

						if not CharConnection then
							CharConnection = LocalPlayerGame.CharacterAdded:Connect(OnCharacterAdded)
						end
					else
						if AnimConnection then
							AnimConnection:Disconnect()
							AnimConnection = nil
						end

						if CharConnection then
							CharConnection:Disconnect()
							CharConnection = nil
						end

						IsExecuting = false
					end
				end,
			})

			SupaTech:Dropdown({
				Title = "Select Abilities",
				Flag = "tsbsupadropdown",
				Values = { "Stream", "Uppercut" },
				Value = { "Stream", "Uppercut" },
				Multi = true,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t324
					SelectedAbilities = arg

					if SupaEnabled then
						SetupAnimationListener()
					end
				end,
			})

			SupaTech:Slider({
				Title = "Dash Delay",
				Flag = "tsbsupadashdelay",
				Value = { Min = 0, Max = 2, Default = 0.3 },
				Step = 0.05,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t319
					DashDelay = arg
				end,
			})

			SupaTech:Slider({
				Title = "Turn Delay",
				Flag = "tsbsupaturndeley",
				Value = { Min = 0, Max = 2, Default = 0.26 },
				Step = 0.05,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t317
					TurnDelay = arg
				end,
			})

			SupaTech:Slider({
				Title = "Stream Pre Delay",
				Flag = "tsbstreamdeley",
				Value = { Min = 0, Max = 5, Default = 2 },
				Step = 0.1,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t318
					StreamPreDelay = arg
				end,
			})

			SupaTech:Keybind({
				Title = "Supa Keybind",
				Flag = "tsbsupakeybind",
				Value = "",
				Callback = function(arg, arg2)
					-- proto t407
					if arg2 and SupaEnabled and not IsExecuting then
						ExecuteSupa(SelectedAbilities[1] or "Uppercut")
					end
				end,
			})

			DownSlam = Tabs.Tech:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "True Downslam Tech", Icon = "box" })
			steps = 10
			duration = 0.1
			TrueDownslamConnection = nil

			DownSlam:Toggle({
				Title = "True DownSlam",
				Flag = "tsbtruedownslamtoggle",
				Desc = "Dont hold M1 click slowly",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t408
					PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
					RunServiceLocal = game:GetService("RunService")
					WorkspaceLocal = game:GetService("Workspace")
					LocalPlayerLocal = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
					CameraLocal = WorkspaceLocal.CurrentCamera

					DownslamAnims = {
						["rbxassetid://13532600125"] = true,
						["rbxassetid://10469630950"] = true,
						["rbxassetid://13296577783"] = true,
						["rbxassetid://13370310513"] = true,
						["rbxassetid://15240216931"] = true,
						["rbxassetid://16515520431"] = true,
						["rbxassetid://17889461810"] = true,
					}

					DownslamHitAnims = {
						["rbxassetid://13532604085"] = true,
						["rbxassetid://10469639222"] = true,
						["rbxassetid://13295919399"] = true,
						["rbxassetid://13378751717"] = true,
						["rbxassetid://15240176873"] = true,
						["rbxassetid://16515448089"] = true,
						["rbxassetid://17889471098"] = true,
					}

					AnimCooldowns = {}
					CharacterLocal = nil

					GetCharacter = function()
						-- proto t411
						char = LocalPlayerLocal.Character or LocalPlayerLocal.CharacterAdded:Wait()
						hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
						char.PrimaryPart = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						return char, hum
					end

					PerformTrueDownslam = function()
						-- proto t412
						if CharacterLocal and CharacterLocal.PrimaryPart then
							camSubject = CameraLocal.CameraSubject
							CameraLocal.CameraSubject = nil
							currentPivot = CharacterLocal:GetPivot()
							targetPivot = currentPivot + Vector3.new(0, 7, 0)

							for i = 1, steps do
								CharacterLocal:PivotTo(currentPivot:Lerp(targetPivot, i / steps))
								task.wait(duration / steps)
							end

							CameraLocal.CameraSubject = camSubject
						end
					end

					if arg then
						if TrueDownslamConnection then
							TrueDownslamConnection:Disconnect()
						end

						local v, v2 = GetCharacter()
						char = v
						hum = v2
						CharacterLocal = char

						TrueDownslamConnection = RunServiceLocal.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
							-- proto t409
							if LocalPlayerLocal.Character ~= CharacterLocal then
								local v3, v4 = GetCharacter()
								char = v3
								hum = v4
								CharacterLocal = char
							end

							for _, v3 in pairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
								animId = tostring(v3.Animation.AnimationId)
								currentTime = tick()

								if DownslamAnims[animId] and (not AnimCooldowns[animId] or currentTime - AnimCooldowns[animId] > 0.5) then
									AnimCooldowns[animId] = currentTime
									task.delay(0.15, PerformTrueDownslam)
								elseif DownslamHitAnims[animId] and (not AnimCooldowns[animId] or currentTime - AnimCooldowns[animId] > 0.5) then
									AnimCooldowns[animId] = currentTime

									task.delay(0.15, function()
										-- proto t410
										hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
									end)
								end
							end
						end)
					elseif TrueDownslamConnection then

					end
				end,
			})

			UpperGraspDelay = 0.18

							-- proto t418

								isAnimating = true
								hasFired = true
								onCooldown = true

								task.delay(UpperGraspDelay, function()
									-- proto t419

									closestDist = 7
									liveFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")

										for _, child in ipairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do

													if dist <= closestDist then
														closestDist = dist
														closestTorso = torso
													end
												end
											end
										end
									end

										targetPos = closestTorso.Position + UpperGraspOffset
										tween = TweenService:Create(rootPart, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos) })
										tween:Play()
										tween.Completed:Wait()
									end

									graspTool = LocalPlayerGame.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Hunter's Grasp")
									communicate = char:FindFirstChild("Communicate")

									if graspTool and communicate then
										communicate:FireServer(unpack({ { Tool = graspTool, Goal = "Console Move" } }))
									end

									isAnimating = false

									task.delay(15, function()
										-- proto t420
										onCooldown = false
									end)
								end)
							elseif not foundAnim then
								hasFired = false
							end
						end)
					end
				end,
			})

			gojo = Tabs.Tech:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Zenci Tech", Icon = "box" })
			UpperDashPitch = 0
			UpperDashTpStartDelay = 0.18
			UpperDashDashDelay = 0.5
			UpperDashUpwards = 8
			UpperDashTpDuration = 1
			UpperDashAnimId = "rbxassetid://10503381238"
			UpperDashEnabled = false
			UpperDashConnection = nil

			gojo:Toggle({
				Title = "Upper + Dash",
				Flag = "tsbupperdashtoggle",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t310
					UpperDashEnabled = arg

					if UpperDashConnection then
						UpperDashConnection:Disconnect()
						UpperDashConnection = nil
					end

					if UpperDashEnabled then
						isAnimating = false
						hasFired = false
						onCooldown = false

						UpperDashConnection = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
							-- proto t311
							char = LocalPlayerGame.Character
							rootPart = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
							humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
							if not (char and rootPart and humanoid) then
								return
							end
							foundAnim = false

							for _, v in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
								if v.Animation and v.Animation.AnimationId == UpperDashAnimId then
									foundAnim = true
									break
								end
							end

							local flag = foundAnim

							if flag then
								flag = not (isAnimating or hasFired or onCooldown)
							end

							if flag then
								isAnimating = true
								hasFired = true
								onCooldown = true
								local cFrame = rootPart.CFrame

								task.delay(UpperDashDashDelay, function()
									-- proto t314
									args = { { Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q, Goal = "KeyPress" } }
									local character_ = LocalPlayerGame.Character

									if character_ and character_:FindFirstChild("Communicate") then
										character_.Communicate:FireServer(unpack(args))
									end
								end)

								task.delay(UpperDashTpStartDelay, function()
									-- proto t312
									closestTorso = nil
									closestDist = 7
									liveFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")

									if liveFolder then
										for _, child in ipairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do
											if child:IsA("Model") and child ~= char then
												torso = child:FindFirstChild("Torso") or child:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")

												if torso then
													dist = (cFrame.Position - torso.Position).Magnitude

													if dist <= closestDist then
														closestDist = dist
														closestTorso = torso
													end
												end
											end
										end
									end

									if closestTorso then
										local now = tick()

										while true do
											local v = UpperDashTpDuration

											if not (tick() - now <= v) then
												break
											else
												if not (not closestTorso or not closestTorso.Parent) then
													targetPos = closestTorso.Position + Vector3.new(0, UpperDashUpwards, 0)
													local cframe = CFrame.Angles
													local cFrame2 = CFrame.new(targetPos) * cframe(math.rad(UpperDashPitch), 0, 0)

													if rootPart then
														rootPart.CFrame = cFrame2
													end

													task.wait()
													continue
												end

												break
											end
										end
									end

									if rootPart then
										rootPart.CFrame = cFrame
									end

									isAnimating = false

									task.delay(15, function()
										-- proto t313
										onCooldown = false
									end)
								end)
							elseif not foundAnim then
								hasFired = false
							end
						end)
					end
				end,
			})

			VIM_HD = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
			TS_HD = game:GetService("TweenService")
			HD_Enabled = false
			HD_Locked = nil
			HD_LockKey = Enum.KeyCode.K
			HD_DashKey = Enum.KeyCode.C
			HD_Side = "Left"
			HD_MaxDist = 50
			HD_Offset = 4
			HD_Hold = 0.18
			HD_TweenTime = 0.18
			HD_M1Delay = 0.12
			HD_AnimSpeed = 1
			HD_QDelay = 0.05
			HD_QHold = 0.06
			HD_Busy = false
			HD_Highlight = nil
			HD_AnimCache = {}

			local function fn(arg)
				-- proto t237
				if not arg then
					return nil
				end
				return arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or arg:FindFirstChild("Torso")
			end

			local function fn2(arg)
				-- proto t238
				if not arg or not arg.Parent then
					return false
				end
				local humanoid_ = arg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				return humanoid_ and humanoid_.Health > 0
			end

			local function fn3()
				-- proto t239
				if HD_Highlight then
					HD_Highlight:Destroy()
					HD_Highlight = nil
				end
			end

			local function fn4(adornee)
				-- proto t228
				fn3()
				if not adornee then
					return
				end
				local ok, result = pcall(Instance.new, "Highlight")

				if ok and result then
					result.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(140, 60, 255)
					result.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(200, 120, 255)
					result.FillTransparency = 0.7
					result.OutlineTransparency = 0.2
					result.Adornee = adornee
					result.Parent = CoreGui
					HD_Highlight = result
				else
					local selectionBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
					selectionBox.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 60, 255)
					selectionBox.LineThickness = 0.04
					selectionBox.SurfaceTransparency = 0.9
					selectionBox.SurfaceColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 0, 255)
					selectionBox.Adornee = adornee
					selectionBox.Parent = CoreGui
					HD_Highlight = selectionBox
				end
			end

			local function fn5()
				-- proto t229
				local character_ = LocalPlayer.Character
				character_ = character_ and character_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not character_ then
					return
				end
				local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
				if not live then
					return
				end
				local v = HD_MaxDist
				local v2 = nil

				for _, child in ipairs(live:GetChildren()) do
					if child.Name ~= LocalPlayer.Name and child:IsA("Model") then
						local humanoid_ = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						local v3 = fn(child)

						if humanoid_ and humanoid_.Health > 0 and v3 then
							local magnitude = (character_.Position - v3.Position).Magnitude

							if magnitude < v then
								v = magnitude
								v2 = child
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if v2 then
					HD_Locked = v2
					fn4(v2)
					lib:Notify({ Title = "Hook Dash", Content = "Locked: " .. v2.Name, Duration = 1.5, Icon = "crosshair" })
				else
					HD_Locked = nil
					fn3()

					lib:Notify({
						Title = "Hook Dash",
						Content = "No target within " .. HD_MaxDist .. " studs",
						Duration = 1.5,
						Icon = "x-circle",
					})
				end
			end

			local function fn6()
				-- proto t230
				local character_ = LocalPlayer.Character
				if not character_ then
					return
				end
				local humanoid_ = character_:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if not humanoid_ then
					return
				end
				local animator = humanoid_:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

				if not animator then
					animator = Instance.new("Animator")
					animator.Parent = humanoid_
				end

				local animation = HD_AnimCache[10480793962]

				if not animation then
					animation = Instance.new("Animation")
					animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://10480793962"
					HD_AnimCache[10480793962] = animation
				end

				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					-- proto t233
					return animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
				end)

				if not ok or not result then
					return
				end
				result.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
				result:Play()

				pcall(function()
					-- proto t231
					result:AdjustSpeed(HD_AnimSpeed)
				end)
			end

			local function fn7(arg)
				-- proto t220
				local a = arg == "Left" and Enum.KeyCode.A or Enum.KeyCode.D
				task.spawn(fn6)
				VIM_HD:SendKeyEvent(true, a, false, game)

				task.delay(HD_QDelay, function()
					-- proto t222
					VIM_HD:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
					task.wait(HD_QHold)
					VIM_HD:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
				end)

				task.delay(HD_Hold, function()
					-- proto t221
					VIM_HD:SendKeyEvent(false, a, false, game)
				end)
			end

			local function fn8()
				-- proto t224
				if HD_Busy then
					return
				end
				local character_ = LocalPlayer.Character
				character_ = character_ and character_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not character_ then
					return
				end

				if not HD_Locked or not fn2(HD_Locked) then
					fn5()
					if not HD_Locked then
						return
					end
				end

				local v = fn(HD_Locked)
				if not v then
					return
				end

				if HD_MaxDist < (character_.Position - v.Position).Magnitude then
					lib:Notify({ Title = "Hook Dash", Content = "Too far — get closer", Duration = 1.5, Icon = "alert-circle" })
					return
				end
				HD_Busy = true
				local vector = Vector3.new(v.Position.X - character_.Position.X, 0, v.Position.Z - character_.Position.Z)

				if vector.Magnitude > 0.1 then
					character_.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(character_.Position, character_.Position + vector)
				end

				fn7(HD_Side)
				task.wait(HD_Hold + 0.12)
				local v2 = fn(HD_Locked)
				if not v2 then
					HD_Busy = false
					return
				end
				local cFrame = v2.CFrame
				local n = cFrame.Position + cFrame.LookVector * -HD_Offset
				local vector2 = Vector3.new(cFrame.Position.X, n.Y, cFrame.Position.Z)
				local cframe = CFrame.lookAt(n, vector2)
				local tween_ = TS_HD:Create(character_, TweenInfo.new(HD_TweenTime, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { CFrame = cframe })
				tween_:Play()
				tween_.Completed:Wait()
				local v3 = fn(HD_Locked)

				if v3 then
					local position = character_.Position
					local vector3 = Vector3.new(v3.Position.X, position.Y, v3.Position.Z)

					if (position - vector3).Magnitude > 0.1 then
						character_.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(position, vector3)
					end
				end

				local v4 = fn(HD_Locked)

				if v4 then
					local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

					if currentCamera then
						local position = character_.Position
						local position2 = v4.Position
						local n2 = position + (position - position2).Unit * 6 + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
						local vector3 = Vector3.new(position2.X, position.Y + 1.5, position2.Z)
						currentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(n2, vector3)
					end
				end

				task.wait(HD_M1Delay)

				task.spawn(function()
					-- proto t225
					local character_2 = LocalPlayer.Character
					local communicate_ = character_2 and character_2:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
					if not communicate_ then
						return
					end

					pcall(function()
						-- proto t226
						communicate_:FireServer(unpack({ { Goal = "LeftClick", Mobile = true } }))
					end)

					task.wait(0.1)

					pcall(function()
						-- proto t227
						communicate_:FireServer(unpack({ { Goal = "LeftClickRelease", Mobile = true } }))
					end)
				end)

				HD_Busy = false
			end

			game:GetService("UserInputService").InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
				-- proto t219
				if not gameProcessed then
					gameProcessed = game:GetService("UserInputService"):GetFocusedTextBox()
				end

				if gameProcessed then
					return
				end

				if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
					return
				end

				if not HD_Enabled then
					return
				end

				if input.KeyCode == HD_LockKey then
					fn5()
				elseif input.KeyCode == HD_DashKey then
					task.spawn(fn8)
				end
			end)

			game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				-- proto t240
				if HD_Locked and not fn2(HD_Locked) then
					HD_Locked = nil
					fn3()
				end
			end)

			HookDashMultiSection = Tabs.Tech:MultiSection({ Title = "Hook Dash", Icon = "fishing-hook", Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true })
			local v = HookDashMultiSection:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Controls", Icon = "zap" })

			v:Toggle({

			local HD_LockGui_M = nil
			local HD_DashGui_M = nil

			local function createScreenGui(name, text, color, arg, arg2, arg3)
				-- proto t256
				local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
				screenGui.Name = name
				screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
				screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
				screenGui.DisplayOrder = 9999
				screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
				screenGui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
				local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
				frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 120, 0, 54)
				frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, arg, 0, arg2)
				frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 16)
				frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
				frame.Active = true
				frame.Parent = screenGui
				Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)
				local uiStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
				uiStroke.Color = color
				uiStroke.Thickness = 1.4
				uiStroke.Transparency = 0.3
				uiStroke.Parent = frame
				local frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")
				frame2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 28)
				frame2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -14)
				frame2.BackgroundColor3 = color
				frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
				frame2.Parent = frame
				Instance.new("UICorner", frame2).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
				local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
				textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -26, 1, 0)
				textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 0)
				textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				textLabel.Text = text
				textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 240)
				textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				textLabel.TextSize = 13
				textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
				textLabel.Parent = frame
				local textButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
				textButton.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
				textButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				textButton.Text = ""
				textButton.ZIndex = 3
				textButton.AutoButtonColor = false
				textButton.Parent = frame
				local color2 = Color3.new(color.R * 0.25, color.G * 0.25, color.B * 0.25)
				local color3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 16)

				textButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
					-- proto t261
					if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
						local tbl = { BackgroundColor3 = color2 }
						TS_HD:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.07, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), tbl):Play()
						uiStroke.Transparency = 0
					end
				end)

				textButton.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
					-- proto t262
					if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
						local tbl = { BackgroundColor3 = color3 }
						TS_HD:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), tbl):Play()
						uiStroke.Transparency = 0.3
						task.spawn(arg3)
					end
				end)

				local flag = false
				local position = nil
				local vector2 = nil

				frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
					-- proto t257
					if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
						flag = true
						position = input.Position
						vector2 = Vector2.new(frame.Position.X.Offset, frame.Position.Y.Offset)

						input.Changed:Connect(function()
							-- proto t258
							if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
								flag = false
							end
						end)
					end
				end)

				frame.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
					-- proto t260
					if not flag then
						return
					end

					if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
						local n = input.Position - position
						frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, vector2.X + n.X, 0, vector2.Y + n.Y)
					end
				end)

				return screenGui
			end

	LagTab = function()
		-- proto t1749
		task.spawn(function()
			-- proto t10650
			game:GetService("Lighting")
			game:GetService("Settings")
			game:GetService("StarterGui")
			game:GetService("RunService")
			workspace:WaitForChild("Thrown")
			workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
			local v = Tabs.Lag:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Performance Optimizations", Icon = "zap" })

			v:Button({
				Title = "Optimize Game Performance",
				Callback = function()
					-- proto t433
					lighting.GlobalShadows = false
					lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
					lighting.Brightness = 0
					settingsService.Rendering.QualityLevel = "Level01"
					local terrain = workspace.Terrain
					terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
					terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
					terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
					terrain.WaterTransparency = 0
					local n = 0

					for _, descendant in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
							if descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
								descendant.Material = "Plastic"
								descendant.Reflectance = 0
								n += 1
							elseif descendant:IsA("Decal") or descendant:IsA("Texture") then
								descendant.Transparency = 1
								n += 1
							end
						elseif descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant:IsA("Trail") then
							descendant.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
							n += 1
						elseif descendant:IsA("Fire") or descendant:IsA("SpotLight") or descendant:IsA("Smoke") or descendant:IsA("Sparkles") then
							descendant.Enabled = false
							n += 1
						end
					end

					starterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "Performance Optimized", Text = string.format("Optimized %d objects", n), Duration = 3 })
				end,
			})

			v:Toggle({
				Title = "Remove Debris & Thrown",
				Flag = "tsbremovedebris",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t434
					if arg then
						thrownChildAddedConnection = thrownFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
							-- proto t437
							child.Parent = nil
						end)

						debrisDescendantAddedConnection = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
							-- proto t435
							if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.Name:lower():match("^debris") then
								descendant.Parent = nil
							end
						end)

						cleanupHeartbeatConnection = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
							-- proto t436
							if #thrownFolder:GetChildren() > 0 then
								thrownFolder:ClearAllChildren()
							end
						end)
					else
						if thrownChildAddedConnection then
							thrownChildAddedConnection:Disconnect()
							thrownChildAddedConnection = nil
						end

						if debrisDescendantAddedConnection then
							debrisDescendantAddedConnection:Disconnect()
							debrisDescendantAddedConnection = nil
						end

						if cleanupHeartbeatConnection then
							cleanupHeartbeatConnection:Disconnect()
							cleanupHeartbeatConnection = nil
						end
					end
				end,
			})

			local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")

			local function fn(arg, canCollide)
				-- proto t438
				if not map then
					return
				end

				for _, descendant in ipairs(map:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant.Name == arg and (descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("Decal")) then
						descendant.Transparency = canCollide and 0 or 1

						if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
							descendant.CanCollide = canCollide
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local v2 = Tabs.Lag:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Hide Parts", Icon = "package" })

			if map then
				v2:Toggle({
					Title = "Trees",
					Flag = "tsbtreesremove",
					Value = true,
					Callback = function(arg)
						-- proto t439
						fn("Trees", arg)
					end,
				})

				v2:Toggle({
					Title = "Walls",
					Flag = "tsbwallsremove",
					Value = true,
					Callback = function(arg)
						-- proto t432
						fn("Walls", arg)
					end,
				})

				v2:Toggle({
					Title = "Grass",
					Flag = "tsbgrassremove",
					Value = true,
					Callback = function(arg)
						-- proto t429
						fn("Grass", arg)
						fn("GrassBottom", arg)
					end,
				})

				v2:Toggle({
					Title = "Benches",
					Flag = "tsbbenchesremove",
					Value = true,
					Callback = function(arg)
						-- proto t430
						fn("Benches", arg)
					end,
				})

				task.spawn(function()
					-- proto t431
					fn("Trees", false)
					fn("Walls", false)
					fn("Grass", false)
					fn("GrassBottom", false)
					fn("Benches", false)
				end)
			end
		end)
	end

	AnimationTab = function()
		-- proto t2253
		task.spawn(function()
			-- proto t10761
			local localPlayer_ = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
			game:GetService("StarterGui")

			local tbl = {
				noAnim = {
					enabled = false,
					connection = nil,
					deathConnection = nil,
					ignoreAnimationId = "136370737633649",
				},
				logger = {
					enabled = false,
					loggedAnims = {},
					dropdownList = {},
					selectedAnimId = nil,
					dropdownObj = nil,
				},
			}

			local function fn()
				-- proto t455
				local character_ = localPlayer_.Character
				if not character_ then
					return
				end
				local humanoid_ = character_:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				if not humanoid_ then
					return
				end

				for _, v in ipairs(humanoid_:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
					v:Stop()
				end
			end

			local fn2 = nil

			fn2 = function()
				-- proto t451
				local character_ = localPlayer_.Character
				if not character_ then
					return
				end
				local humanoid_ = character_:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or character_:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

				if tbl.noAnim.connection then
					tbl.noAnim.connection:Disconnect()
				end

				if tbl.noAnim.deathConnection then
					tbl.noAnim.deathConnection:Disconnect()
				end

				tbl.noAnim.connection = humanoid_.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(arg)
					-- proto t454
					local match = arg.Animation and arg.Animation.AnimationId:match("%d+")

					if match and match ~= tbl.noAnim.ignoreAnimationId then
						arg:Stop()
					end
				end)

				tbl.noAnim.deathConnection = humanoid_.Died:Connect(function()
					-- proto t452
					fn2()
				end)
			end

			Tabs.Anim:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Play/Stop", Icon = "eye" }):Toggle({
				Title = "No Animation",
				Flag = "tsbnoanimtoggle",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(enabled)
					-- proto t449
					tbl.noAnim.enabled = enabled

					if enabled then
						fn2()
					else
						if tbl.noAnim.connection then
							tbl.noAnim.connection:Disconnect()
							tbl.noAnim.connection = nil
						end

						if tbl.noAnim.deathConnection then
							tbl.noAnim.deathConnection:Disconnect()
							tbl.noAnim.deathConnection = nil
						end
					end
				end,
			})

			local v = Tabs.Anim:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Animation Logger", Icon = "scroll-text" })

			local function fn3()
				-- proto t450
				tbl.logger.dropdownObj:Refresh(#tbl.logger.dropdownList > 0 and tbl.logger.dropdownList or { "No Animation Logged Yet.." })
			end

			local function fn4(arg)
				-- proto t461
				if not tbl.logger.enabled then
					return
				end
				local animation = arg.Animation
				if not animation then
					return
				end
				local match = animation.AnimationId:match("%d+")
				if not match then
					return
				end

				if not tbl.logger.loggedAnims[match] then
					tbl.logger.loggedAnims[match] = true
					table.insert(tbl.logger.dropdownList, ((animation.Name == "Animation" or animation.Name == "") and "Anim" or animation.Name) .. " | ID: " .. match)
					fn3()
				end
			end

			local function fn5(character_)
				-- proto t462
				if not character_ then
					return
				end
				local humanoid_ = character_:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or character_:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
				if not humanoid_ then
					return
				end
				humanoid_.AnimationPlayed:Connect(fn4)
			end

			if localPlayer_.Character then
				fn5(localPlayer_.Character)
			end

			localPlayer_.CharacterAdded:Connect(fn5)

			v:Toggle({
				Title = "Enable Animation Logger",
				Flag = "tsbanimloggertoggle",
				Desc = "Turn this on to start detecting animations.",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(enabled)
					-- proto t459
					tbl.logger.enabled = enabled

					if enabled then
						lib:Notify({ Title = "Logger", Content = "Logging Started", Duration = 1 })
					else
						lib:Notify({ Title = "Logger", Content = "Logging Paused", Duration = 1 })
					end
				end,
			})

			tbl.logger.dropdownObj = v:Dropdown({
				Title = "Logged Animations",
				Desc = "List of animations played on your character",
				Value = "No Animation Logged Yet..",
				Options = { "Nothing to see..." },
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t458
					local match = arg:match("ID: (%d+)")

					if match then
						tbl.logger.selectedAnimId = match
					end
				end,
			})

			v:Button({
				Title = "Copy Selected ID",
				Desc = "Copies the ID of the selected animation.",
				Callback = function()
					-- proto t460
					local selectedAnimId = tbl.logger.selectedAnimId

					if selectedAnimId then
						setclipboard(tostring(selectedAnimId))
						lib:Notify({ Title = "Copied", Content = "ID: " .. selectedAnimId, Duration = 2 })
					else
						lib:Notify({
							Title = "Animation Logger",
							Content = "Select an animation from the list please..",
							Duration = 2,
						})
					end
				end,
			})

			v:Button({
				Title = "Play Selected ID",
				Desc = "Plays the selected animation on your character.",
				Callback = function()
					-- proto t457
					local selectedAnimId = tbl.logger.selectedAnimId

					if selectedAnimId then
						local character_ = localPlayer_.Character

						if character_ and character_:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
							local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
							animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. selectedAnimId
							character_.Humanoid:LoadAnimation(animation):Play()
							lib:Notify({ Title = "Animation Logger", Content = "Playing Animation..", Duration = 2 })
						end
					else
						lib:Notify({
							Title = "Animation Logger",
							Content = "Select an animation from the list please..",
							Duration = 2,
						})
					end
				end,
			})

			v:Button({
				Title = "Stop All Animations",
				Desc = "Stops all playing animations.",
				Callback = function()
					-- proto t463
					fn()
					lib:Notify({ Title = "Animation Logger", Content = "All Animations Stopped", Duration = 2 })
				end,
			})

			v:Button({
				Title = "Clear List",
				Desc = "Removes all logged animations.",
				Callback = function()
					-- proto t456
					tbl.logger.loggedAnims = {}
					tbl.logger.dropdownList = {}
					tbl.logger.selectedAnimId = nil
					fn3()
					tbl.logger.dropdownObj:SetValue("No Animation Logged Yet..")
					lib:Notify({ Title = "Cleared", Content = "List has been reset.", Duration = 2 })
				end,
			})

			CharAnimsSec = Tabs.Anim:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Characters", Icon = "user" })

			for _, v2 in ipairs({
				{ "Saitama", "Saitama Animations", "https://pastefy.app/MSphhjVN/raw" },
				{ "Garou", "Garou Animations", "https://pastefy.app/1w9avWlH/raw" },
				{ "Genos", "Genos Animations", "https://pastefy.app/MA2R0RGL/raw" },
				{ "Sonic", "Sonic Animations", "https://pastefy.app/wAnBZuuG/raw" },
				{ "MetalBat", "MetalBat Animations", "https://pastefy.app/N2pa00Ze/raw" },
				{ "Atomic Samurai", "Atomic Samurai Animations", "https://pastefy.app/wB3HaA45/raw" },
				{ "Tatsumaki", "Tatsumaki Animations", "https://pastefy.app/U1j3SLwL/raw" },
				{ "Suiryu", "Suiryu Animations", "https://pastefy.app/YgSfUw3l/raw" },
				{ "Child Emperor", "Child Emperor Animations", "https://pastefy.app/p1whAqk6/raw" },
				{ "Fracture Boss", "Fracture Boss Animations", "https://pastefy.app/DN4SW5Q1/raw" },
				{ "Gojo", "Gojo Animations", "https://pastefy.app/T96X6meh/raw" },
				{ "Dummy", "Dummy Animations", "https://pastefy.app/tZCD17IH/raw" },
				{ "KJ", "KJ Animations", "https://pastefy.app/2syR66Gd/raw" },
			}) do
				CharAnimsSec:Button({
					Title = v2[2],
					Locked = false,
					Callback = function()
						-- proto t443
						loadstring(game:HttpGet(v2[3]))()
					end,
				})
			end
		end)
	end

	DisguiseTab = function()
		-- proto t2080
		task.spawn(function()
			-- proto t10692
			local Players_ = game:GetService("Players")
			local localPlayer_ = Players_.LocalPlayer
			local tbl = { meshList = {} }

			local function fn(arg)
				-- proto t11034
				if not arg then
					return
				end
				local head = arg:FindFirstChild("Head")
				local torso_ = arg:FindFirstChild("Torso")

				for _, v in pairs(tbl.meshList) do
					if v and v.Parent then
						pcall(function()
							-- proto t469
							v:Destroy()
						end)
					end
				end

				tbl.meshList = {}
				local tbl2 = { "Accessory", "Shirt", "Pants", "CharacterMesh", "BodyColors", "ShirtGraphic" }

				for _, child in pairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
					for _, v in pairs(tbl2) do
						if child:IsA(v) then
							child:Destroy()
							break
						end
					end
				end

				if head then
					for _, child in pairs(head:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") or child:IsA("Decal") or child:IsA("MeshPart") then
							child:Destroy()
						end
					end
				end

				if torso_ then
					for _, child in pairs(torso_:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") or child:IsA("MeshPart") then
							child:Destroy()
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local function fn2(parent, arg)
				-- proto t11032
				if not parent or not arg then
					return
				end
				local head = parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
				local torso_ = parent:FindFirstChild("Torso")

				for _, child in pairs(arg:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Shirt") or child:IsA("Pants") or child:IsA("BodyColors") or child:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then
						child:Clone().Parent = parent
					elseif child:IsA("Accessory") then
						local clone_ = child:Clone()
						clone_.Name = "#ACCESSORY_" .. clone_.Name
						clone_.Parent = parent
					elseif child:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
						local clone_ = child:Clone()

						if child.MeshType then
							clone_.MeshType = child.MeshType
						end

						if child.Scale then
							clone_.Scale = child.Scale
						end

						if child.MeshId then
							clone_.MeshId = child.MeshId
						end

						if child.TextureId then
							clone_.TextureId = child.TextureId
						end

						if child.VertexColor then
							clone_.VertexColor = child.VertexColor
						end

						table.insert(tbl.meshList, clone_)
						local parent2 = child.Parent and child.Parent.Name == "Torso" and torso_ or head

						if parent2 then
							clone_.Parent = parent2
						end
					elseif child.Name == "R6" then
						for _, child2 in pairs(child:GetChildren()) do
							if child2:IsA("CharacterMesh") then
								child2:Clone().Parent = parent
							end
						end
					elseif child.Name == "face" and child:IsA("Decal") then
						child:Clone().Parent = head
					elseif child:IsA("MeshPart") then
						local clone_ = child:Clone()
						clone_.CanCollide = false
						clone_.Anchored = false
						local str = child.Name:lower()

						if str:find("head") then
							clone_.Parent = head
						elseif str:find("torso") or str:find("upper") or str:find("lower") then
							clone_.Parent = torso_ or parent
						else
							clone_.Parent = parent
						end

						table.insert(tbl.meshList, clone_)
					end
				end

				if head then
					if not head:FindFirstChild("face") then
						local decal = Instance.new("Decal")
						decal.Name = "face"
						decal.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front
						decal.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/face.png"
						decal.Parent = head
					end

					if not head:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh") then
						local specialMesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
						specialMesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.Head
						specialMesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1.25, 1.25, 1.25)
						specialMesh.Parent = head
						table.insert(tbl.meshList, specialMesh)
					end
				end
			end

			local function fn3(arg)
				-- proto t11033
				local character_ = localPlayer_.Character
				if not character_ then
					return
				end
				fn(character_)

					-- proto t471

				character_.Parent = parent
			end

				-- proto t11031

				character_.Parent = parent
			end

			local function fn15(character_)
				-- proto t559
				character_.AttributeChanged:Connect(function(attribute)
					-- proto t562
					if attribute == "Combo" and character_:GetAttribute("Combo") == 5 and fn11(character_) then
						if tbl7.Anywhere.Value then
							if tbl7.Bring.Value then
								tbl4["Doing Wall Combo Anywhere"] = true
								local now = tick()

								repeat
									getgenv().vexonWallDesync = {
										CFrame = fn11(character_).CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(-1.5707963267948966, 0, 0),
									}

									task.wait()
								until now + 0.225 <= tick()

								local cFrame3 = fn11(character_).CFrame
								local vexonWallDesync = { CFrame = tbl5[tbl8.Area.Value] }
								getgenv().vexonWallDesync = vexonWallDesync
								task.wait(0.2)
								fn14({ Goal = "Wall Combo" })
								getgenv().vexonWallDesync = nil
								tbl4["Doing Wall Combo Anywhere"] = false
								task.wait(0.5)

								if character_:FindFirstChild("ForceField") and tbl7.TPBack.Value then
									fn13(cFrame3)
								end
							else
								tbl4["Doing Wall Combo Anywhere"] = true
								local now = tick()

								repeat
									getgenv().vexonWallDesync = {
										CFrame = fn11(character_).CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -0.5, 0) * CFrame.Angles(-1.5707963267948966, 0, 0),
									}

									task.wait()
								until now + 0.6 <= tick()

								getgenv().vexonWallDesync = nil
								tbl4["Doing Wall Combo Anywhere"] = false
							end
						end
					end
				end)

				character_.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
					-- proto t563
					if descendant:IsA("ObjectValue") and descendant.Name:lower() == "wallcombo" then
						local now = tick()

						while true do
							if tbl7.Auto.Value then
								fn14({ Goal = "Wall Combo" })
							end

							task.wait()
							local flag5 = descendant.Parent ~= character_

							if not flag5 then
								flag5 = tick() >= now + (descendant:GetAttribute("DeleteMe") or 0.6)
							end

							if not flag5 then
								continue
							end
							break
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			local function fn16(cFrame3, arg)
				-- proto t490
				if not cFrame3 then
					return
				end
				local character_ = localPlayer_2.Character
				if not character_ then
					return
				end
				local humanoidRootPart_ = character_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character_:FindFirstChild("Torso")
				if not humanoidRootPart_ or not humanoidRootPart_.Parent then
					return
				end

				if UseAttachTeleport then
					local cFrame4 = cFrame3 * CFrame.new(0, AttachModeEnabled and 1.5 or 3.5, 0)
					humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = cFrame4

					pcall(function()
						-- proto t493
						character_:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(cFrame4)
					end)

					local head = nil

					if arg and arg.Character then
						head = arg.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or arg.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					end

					if head and head.Parent then
						pcall(function()
							-- proto t492
							sethiddenproperty(humanoidRootPart_, "PhysicsRepRootPart", head)
						end)
					end
				else
					humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = cFrame3
				end
			end

			local function fn17()
				-- proto t495
				local character_ = localPlayer_2.Character
				character_ = character_ and character_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not character_ then
					return
				end
				local tbl9 = {}

				for _, player_ in ipairs(Players_2:GetPlayers()) do
					if player_ ~= localPlayer_2 and player_.Character and player_.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
						if (player_.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - character_.Position).Magnitude > 15 and player_.Name ~= LastBroughtPlayer then
							table.insert(tbl9, player_)
						end
					end
				end

				if #tbl9 == 0 then
					for _, player_ in ipairs(Players_2:GetPlayers()) do
						if player_ ~= localPlayer_2 and player_.Character and player_.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
							if (player_.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - character_.Position).Magnitude > 15 then
								table.insert(tbl9, player_)
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if #tbl9 > 0 then
					local v3 = tbl9[math.random(1, #tbl9)]
					LastBroughtPlayer = v3.Name
					SelectedTarget = v3.Name
					BringPlayer(SelectedTarget)
				end
			end

			SetNoclip = function(arg)
				-- proto t496
				local character_ = localPlayer_2.Character

				if character_ and character_.PrimaryPart then
					if NoclipConnection then
						NoclipConnection:Disconnect()
						NoclipConnection = nil
					end

					local collisionGroup = arg and str or "Default"

					for _, descendant in ipairs(character_:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
							descendant.CollisionGroup = collisionGroup
						end
					end

					if arg then
						NoclipConnection = RunService_2.Stepped:Connect(function()
							-- proto t497
							if character_ then
								for _, descendant in ipairs(character_:GetDescendants()) do
									if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
										descendant.CanCollide = false
									end
								end
							end
						end)

						if BodyPosition then
							BodyPosition:Destroy()
						end

						BodyPosition = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
						BodyPosition.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
						BodyPosition.P = 50000
						BodyPosition.D = 1200
						BodyPosition.Parent = character_.PrimaryPart
					elseif BodyPosition then
						BodyPosition:Destroy()
						BodyPosition = nil
					end
				end
			end

			BringPlayer = function(arg)
				-- proto t498
				local v3 = Players_2:FindFirstChild(arg)

				if v3 and v3 ~= localPlayer_2 and v3.Character then
					task.spawn(function()
						-- proto t499
						local character_ = localPlayer_2.Character

						if character_ and character_.PrimaryPart and v3.Character then
							local communicate_ = character_:FindFirstChild("Communicate")

							if communicate_ and communicate_:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
								IsBringing = true

								if ReturnToOldPosition then
									OldPositionCFrame = character_.PrimaryPart.CFrame
								end

								local cFrame3 = nil

								if SelectedLocation == "Current Location" then
									cFrame3 = character_.PrimaryPart.CFrame
								end

								HeightOffset = 15
								SetNoclip(true)

								local tbl9 = {
									Click = { { Goal = "LeftClick", Mobile = true } },
									Jump = { { Goal = "Record Jump" } },
									Release = { { Goal = "LeftClickRelease", Mobile = true } },
									WallCombo = { { Goal = "Wall Combo", Mobile = true } },
								}

								communicate_:FireServer(unpack(tbl9.Click))
								task.wait(0.8)
								communicate_:FireServer(unpack(tbl9.Jump))
								task.wait(0.36)
								HeightOffset = 6
								task.wait(0.15)
								HeightOffset = 1
								IsInCombo = true
								task.wait(0.35)
								communicate_:FireServer(unpack(tbl9.Release))
								IsBringing = false
								IsInCombo = false

								if not IsFollowing then
									SetNoclip(false)
								end

								local primaryPart = character_.PrimaryPart

								if not (SelectedLocation == "Current Location" and cFrame3) then
									cFrame3 = Locations[SelectedLocation]
								end

								if primaryPart and cFrame3 then
									fn16(cFrame3, v3)
								end

								task.wait(0.1)

								for i = 1, 6 do
									communicate_:FireServer(unpack(tbl9.WallCombo))
									task.wait(0.03)
								end

								if ReturnToOldPosition and OldPositionCFrame then
									task.wait(0.5)
									fn16(OldPositionCFrame, nil)
									OldPositionCFrame = nil
								end

								task.wait(0.5)
							end
						end
					end)
				end
			end

						if (IsFollowing or IsBringing) and SelectedTarget then

							if not (v3 and v3.Character) then
								return

							if not primaryPart then
								return

							local position

							if UseAttachTeleport then
								position = primaryPart.Position
							else
								position = primaryPart.Position + Vector3.new(primaryPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, primaryPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z) * PredictionTime
							end

							local position2

							if IsInCombo then
								position2 = position - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)

							end

							if BodyPosition then
								BodyPosition.Position = position2
							end
						end
					end
				end

	AttachTab = function()
		-- proto t2109
		task.spawn(function()
			-- proto t10719
			AttachSec = Tabs.Attach:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Attach", Icon = "paperclip" })

			AttachSec:Paragraph({
				Title = "Note:",
				Desc = "its like a loop tp but with no cooldown rly op. Only works for rly good executors like delta if you use executors that not support cloneref will probably just crash. Xeno not Supported!",
				Image = "rbxassetid://84519376661277",
				ImageSize = 30,
				Thumbnail = "",
				ThumbnailSize = 0,
				Locked = false,
			})

			GetService = function(arg)
				-- proto t583
				return cloneref and cloneref(game:GetService(arg)) or game:GetService(arg)
			end

			Players = GetService("Players")
			RunService = GetService("RunService")
			LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
			Character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
			RootPart = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			TargetPlayer = nil
			IsEnabled = false
			Offset = Vector3.zero
			RotOffset = Vector3.zero
			HeartbeatConnection = nil
			OffsetCFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)

			UpdateOffset = function()
				-- proto t588
				local cframe = CFrame.Angles
				local rad = math.rad
				local z = RotOffset.Z
				OffsetCFrame = CFrame.new(Offset.X, Offset.Y, Offset.Z) * cframe(math.rad(RotOffset.X), math.rad(RotOffset.Y), rad(z))
			end

			FindPlayer = function(arg)
				-- proto t589
				local str = arg:lower():gsub("%s", "")
				local v = next
				local players, v2 = Players:GetPlayers()

				for _, v3 in v, players, v2 do
					if v3 ~= LocalPlayer and (v3.Name:lower():match(str) or v3.DisplayName:lower():match("^" .. str)) then
						return v3
					end
				end

				return nil
			end

			UpdateLoop = function()
				-- proto t590
				if not IsEnabled then
					return
				end

				if Character and RootPart then
					if TargetPlayer and TargetPlayer.Character then
						TargetRoot = TargetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						TargetHead = TargetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

						if TargetRoot then
							RootPart.CFrame = TargetRoot.CFrame * OffsetCFrame

							if TargetHead then
								pcall(function()
									-- proto t591
									sethiddenproperty(RootPart, "PhysicsRepRootPart", TargetHead)
								end)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character_)
				-- proto t584
				Character = character_
				RootPart = character_:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			end)

			AttachSec:Input({
				Title = "Target Name",
				Placeholder = "Player Name...",
				Callback = function(arg)

	FightTab = function()
		-- proto t1581
		task.spawn(function()
			-- proto t10632
			AimbotSection = Tabs.Fight:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Aimbot", Icon = "locate-fixed" })
			Players = game:GetService("Players")
			RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
			UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
			CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
			Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
			localplayer = Players.LocalPlayer
			CurrentCamera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
			Mouse = localplayer:GetMouse()
			_G.GunAimbotArgs = nil
			aimbotEnabled = false
			local g = Enum.KeyCode.G
			predictionOffset = 2.8
			offsetToPingMult = 1
			targetMode = "Nearest"
			wallCheckEnabled = false
			aimType = "Camera"
			aimPart = "Head"

			isVisible = function(arg)
				-- proto t613
				if not wallCheckEnabled then
					return true
				end
				local character_ = localplayer.Character
				local character_2 = arg.Character
				if not character_ or not character_2 then
					return false
				end
				local position = CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position
				local upperTorso = character_2:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not upperTorso then
					return false
				end
				local n = upperTorso.Position - position
				if n.Magnitude <= 0 then
					return true
				end
				local raycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
				raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { character_, character_2 }
				raycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
				raycastParams.IgnoreWater = true
				return workspace:Raycast(position, n, raycastParams) == nil
			end

			local function fn()
				-- proto t614
				local character_ = localplayer.Character
				if not character_ then
					return nil
				end
				local humanoidRootPart_ = character_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not humanoidRootPart_ then
					return nil
				end

				if targetMode == "Nearest" then
					local huge = math.huge
					local v = nil

					for _, player_ in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
						if player_ ~= localplayer then
							local character_2 = player_.Character

							if character_2 then
								local humanoid_ = character_2:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")

								if humanoid_ and humanoid_.Health > 0 then
									local humanoidRootPart_2 = character_2:FindFirstChild(aimPart) or character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

									if humanoidRootPart_2 then
										if isVisible(player_) then
											local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart_2.Position - humanoidRootPart_.Position).Magnitude

											if magnitude < huge then
												huge = magnitude
												v = player_
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					return v
				end

				if targetMode == "Closest to Crosshair" then
					local vector2 = Vector2.new(CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.X / 2, CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
					local huge = math.huge
					local v = nil

					for _, player_ in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
						if player_ ~= localplayer then
							local character_2 = player_.Character

							if character_2 then
								local humanoid_ = character_2:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")

								if humanoid_ and humanoid_.Health > 0 then
									local humanoidRootPart_2 = character_2:FindFirstChild(aimPart) or character_2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

									if humanoidRootPart_2 then
										local v2, v3 = CurrentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(humanoidRootPart_2.Position)

										if v3 and isVisible(player_) then
											local magnitude = (vector2 - Vector2.new(v2.X, v2.Y)).Magnitude

											if magnitude < huge then
												huge = magnitude
												v = player_
											end
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					return v
				end

				return nil
			end

			local function fn2(arg)
				-- proto t615
				local character_ = arg.Character
				if not character_ then
					return nil
				end
				local humanoidRootPart_ = character_:FindFirstChild(aimPart) or character_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not humanoidRootPart_ then
					return nil
				end
				local humanoid_ = character_:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
				if not humanoid_ or humanoid_.Health <= 0 then
					return nil
				end
				velocity = humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity
				moveDir = humanoid_.MoveDirection
				velocityOffset = velocity * Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0) * predictionOffset / 15
				moveOffset = moveDir * predictionOffset
				predOffset = velocityOffset + moveOffset
				ping = localplayer:GetNetworkPing() * 1000
				pingMult = ping * (offsetToPingMult - 1) * 0.01 + 1
				predicted = humanoidRootPart_.Position + predOffset * pingMult
				return predicted
			end

			textButton2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)

			local flag = false
			local v = nil
			local position = nil
			local vector2 = nil

			textButton2.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
				-- proto t616

					flag = true
					position = input.Position

					input.Changed:Connect(function()
						-- proto t618

							flag = false
						end
					end)
				end
			end)

			textButton2.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
				-- proto t619
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
					v = input
				end
			end)

			RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
				-- proto t621
				if flag and v and position and vector2 then
					local n = v.Position - position
					textButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, vector2.X + n.X, 0, vector2.Y + n.Y)
				end

				if not aimbotEnabled then
					_G.GunAimbotArgs = nil
					return
				end
				local v2 = fn()

				if v2 and v2.Character then
					local v3 = fn2(v2)

					if v3 then
						if aimType == "Camera" then
							_G.GunAimbotArgs = { 1, v3, "AH2" }
							CurrentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position, v3)
						elseif aimType == "Character" then
							_G.GunAimbotArgs = nil
							local character_ = localplayer.Character

							if character_ then
								local humanoidRootPart_ = character_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

								if humanoidRootPart_ then
									humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart_.Position, Vector3.new(v3.X, humanoidRootPart_.Position.Y, v3.Z))
								end
							end
						end
					else
						_G.GunAimbotArgs = nil
					end
				else
					_G.GunAimbotArgs = nil
				end
			end)

			local v2 = AimbotSection:Toggle({
				Title = "Main Aimbot Toggle",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t620
					aimbotEnabled = arg

					if not arg then
						_G.GunAimbotArgs = nil
					end

				-- proto t627

					-- proto t608

					end
