			ReachSec:Toggle({
				Title = "M1 Click Reach",
				Flag = "tsbm1clickreachtoggle",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(isEnabled_)
					-- proto t605
					reachSettings.isEnabled = isEnabled_
					lplr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
					liveFolder = workspace:WaitForChild("Live")

					if reachSettings.isEnabled then
						task.spawn(function()
							-- proto t606
							while reachSettings.isEnabled do
								char = lplr.Character
								root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

								if char and root and char:FindFirstChild("M1ing") then
									myPos = root.Position
									nearestRoot = nil
									dist = reachSettings.detectionDistance

									for _, child in ipairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do
										if child ~= char then
											h = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
											hrp = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

											if h and hrp and h.Health > 0 then
												mag = (myPos - hrp.Position).Magnitude

												if mag < dist then
													dist = mag
													nearestRoot = hrp
												end
											end
										end
									end

									if nearestRoot then
										pcall(function()
											-- proto t607
											targetPos = nearestRoot.Position + -nearestRoot.CFrame.LookVector * reachSettings.teleportDistance
											char:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.lookAt(targetPos, nearestRoot.Position))
										end)
									end
								end

								task.wait(0.02)
							end
						end)
					end
				end,
			})

			player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
			running = false

			startClickLoop = function()
				-- proto t602
				task.spawn(function()
					-- proto t603
					while running do
						local character_ = player.Character

						if character_ then
							humanoidRootPart = character_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

							if humanoidRootPart then
								local v3 = pairs
								local Players_ = game:GetService("Players")

								for _, player_ in v3(Players_:GetPlayers()) do
									if player_ ~= player and player_.Character and player_.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
										distance = (humanoidRootPart.Position - player_.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

										if distance < 10 then
											args1 = { { Goal = "LeftClick", Mobile = true } }
											player.Character.Communicate:FireServer(unpack(args1))
											task.wait(0.1)
											args2 = { { Goal = "LeftClickRelease", Mobile = true } }
											player.Character.Communicate:FireServer(unpack(args2))
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end

						task.wait(0.1)
					end
				end)
			end

													isAttacking = true

											end
										end

	FlingTab = function()

			local function fn(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
				-- proto t847
				StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
					Title = arg or "VexonHub",
					Text = arg2,
					Icon = arg3 or "rbxassetid://84519376661277",

			end

			local function fn2(arg)
				-- proto t848
				for _, v in pairs(arg) do
					if typeof(v) == "RBXScriptConnection" and v.Connected then
						v:Disconnect()
					end
				end

				table.clear(arg)
			end

			local function fn3(arg, character_)
				-- proto t849
				arg.Character = character_

				if character_ then
					arg.Root = character_:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
					arg.Head = character_:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
					arg.Humanoid = character_:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
				else
					arg.Root = nil
					arg.Head = nil
					arg.Humanoid = nil
				end
			end

			local function fn4()
				-- proto t810
				if espBillboard then
					espBillboard:Destroy()
					espBillboard = nil

					return
				end

				-- proto t812

			local function fn7(arg)
				-- proto t814
				fn2(connections)
				fn5()
				selectedPlayerObject = nil

					fn("VexonHub", "No One Selected")
					return
				end
				local v = string.lower(arg)

				for _, player_ in ipairs(PlayersService:GetPlayers()) do
					if string.find(string.lower(player_.Name), v, 1, true) or string.find(string.lower(player_.DisplayName), v, 1, true) then

					if selectedPlayerObject.Character then

								fn4()

				end
			end

			local function fn8(cFrame, arg)
				-- proto t822
				if not cFrame then
					return
				end
				local character_ = localPlayer.Character
				if not character_ then
					return
				end
				local humanoidRootPart_ = character_:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not humanoidRootPart_ or not humanoidRootPart_.Parent then
					return
				end

				if USE_ATTACH_TELEPORT then
					local cFrame2 = cFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1.5, 0)
					humanoidRootPart_.CFrame = cFrame2

						-- proto t826
						character_:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(cFrame2)
					end)

					local head = arg

					if targetCache.Head and targetCache.Head.Parent then
						head = targetCache.Head
					end

					if head and head.Parent then
						pcall(function()
							-- proto t825
							sethiddenproperty(humanoidRootPart_, "PhysicsRepRootPart", head)
						end)
					end
				else

				end
			end

			local function fn9()
				-- proto t827
				if not localCache.Root or not localCache.Humanoid or not targetCache.Character or isFlinging then
					return
				end
				local root_ = targetCache.Root
				local head = targetCache.Head
				local v = head or root_
				if not v then
					return
				end
				isFlinging = true

				if localCache.Root.Velocity.Magnitude < 50 then
					tbl.OldPos = localCache.Root.CFrame
				end

				local humanoid_ = targetCache.Humanoid or head

				if humanoid_ then
					camera.CameraSubject = humanoid_
				end

				workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = (0/0)
				local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
				bodyVelocity.Name = "EpixVel"
				bodyVelocity.Parent = localCache.Root
				bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(900000000, 900000000, 900000000)
				bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
				localCache.Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, false)
				local now = tick()
				local n = 0

				flingConnections.flingLoop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
					-- proto t828
					local sit = not v or not v.Parent or v.Velocity.Magnitude > 500 or targetCache.Humanoid and targetCache.Humanoid.Sit or localCache.Humanoid.Health <= 0

					if not sit then
						local n2 = now + 2
						sit = tick() > n2
					end

					if sit then
						fn2(flingConnections)
						bodyVelocity:Destroy()
						localCache.Humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated, true)
						camera.CameraSubject = localCache.Humanoid

						task.delay(0.1, function()
							-- proto t830
							local cFrame = tbl.OldPos and tbl.OldPos * CFrame.new(0, 0.5, 0) or localCache.Root.CFrame
							local n2 = 0
							local connection_ = nil

							connection_ = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
								-- proto t831
								if not localCache.Root then
									connection_:Disconnect()
									return
								end
								localCache.Root.CFrame = cFrame
								localCache.Character:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(cFrame)
								localCache.Humanoid:ChangeState("GettingUp")

								for _, child in ipairs(localCache.Character:GetChildren()) do
									if child:IsA("BasePart") then
										child.Velocity = Vector3.new()
										child.RotVelocity = Vector3.new()
									end
								end

								n2 += 1

								if (localCache.Root.Position - cFrame.Position).Magnitude < 25 or n2 > 50 then
									connection_:Disconnect()
									workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = tbl.FPDH
									isFlinging = false
								end
							end)
						end)

						return
					end

					n += 8000 * deltaTime
					local cframe = USE_ATTACH_TELEPORT and CFrame.new(0, 0.8 * math.sin(tick() * 10), 0) or CFrame.new(0, 1.5 * math.sin(tick() * 10), 0)
					local cframe2 = USE_ATTACH_TELEPORT and CFrame.Angles(math.rad(n), math.rad(n * 0.7), 0) or CFrame.Angles(math.rad(n), 0, 0)

					if not USE_ATTACH_TELEPORT and v.Velocity.Magnitude < 50 and targetCache.Humanoid then
						cframe += targetCache.Humanoid.MoveDirection * v.Velocity.Magnitude / PREDICTION_INTENSITY
					end

					local cFrame = CFrame.new(v.Position) * cframe * cframe2
					localCache.Root.CFrame = cFrame
					localCache.Character:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(cFrame)
					localCache.Root.Velocity = Vector3.new(90000000, 900000000, 90000000)
					localCache.Root.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(900000000, 900000000, 900000000)

					if USE_ATTACH_TELEPORT then
						pcall(function()
							-- proto t829
							sethiddenproperty(localCache.Root, "PhysicsRepRootPart", v)
						end)
					end
				end)
			end

			local function fn10()
				-- proto t838
				toolEquipped = false

				if selectorTool then
					selectorTool:Destroy()
					selectorTool = nil
				end

				local v = localPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(TOOL_NAME)

				if v then
					v:Destroy()
				end

				local character_ = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(TOOL_NAME)

				if character_ then
					character_:Destroy()
				end
			end

			local function fn11()
				-- proto t839
				if not toolToggleEnabled then
					return
				end
				local v = localPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(TOOL_NAME)
				local character_ = localPlayer.Character and localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(TOOL_NAME)
				if v or character_ then
					return
				end
				selectorTool = Instance.new("Tool")
				selectorTool.Name = TOOL_NAME
				selectorTool.RequiresHandle = false
				selectorTool.Parent = localPlayer.Backpack

				connections.toolEquipped = selectorTool.Equipped:Connect(function()
					-- proto t841
					toolEquipped = true
					fn("VexonHub", "Tool Equipped. Tap/Click a player to select.", nil, 3)
				end)

				connections.toolUnequipped = selectorTool.Unequipped:Connect(function()
					-- proto t840
					toolEquipped = false
				end)
			end

			UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
				-- proto t843
				if gameProcessed or not toolToggleEnabled or not toolEquipped then
					return
				end

				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
					local target

					if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
						target = localPlayer:GetMouse().Target
					else
						local v = camera:ViewportPointToRay(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
						local hit = workspace:Raycast(v.Origin, v.Direction * 1000)
						target = nil

						if hit then
							target = hit.Instance
						end
					end

					if target then
						local model = target:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")

						if model then
							local playerFromCharacter = PlayersService:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)

							if playerFromCharacter and playerFromCharacter ~= localPlayer then
								if playerDropdown then
									playerDropdown:Select(playerFromCharacter.Name)
								end
							elseif playerFromCharacter == localPlayer then
								fn("VexonHub", "You cannot select yourself!", nil, 3)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)

			RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
				-- proto t844
				if not localCache.Root or not localCache.Humanoid then
					return
				end

				if tbl2.aimLockCam and targetCache.Head then
					camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(camera.CFrame.Position, targetCache.Head.Position)
				end

				local root_ = targetCache.Root
				local head = targetCache.Head
				root_ = root_ or head
				if not root_ then
					return
				end

				if tbl2.view and targetCache.Humanoid then
					if camera.CameraSubject ~= targetCache.Humanoid then
						camera.CameraSubject = targetCache.Humanoid
					end
				end

				if tbl2.teleport then
					fn8(root_.CFrame, root_)
				elseif tbl2.orbit then
					orbitAngle = (orbitAngle + orbitSpeed * deltaTime * 50) % 360
					local cframe = CFrame.Angles
					local position = root_.Position
					fn8(CFrame.new((CFrame.new(root_.Position) * cframe(0, math.rad(orbitAngle), 0) * CFrame.new(orbitDistance, 0, 0)).Position, position), root_)
				elseif tbl2.aimLockChar then
					fn8(CFrame.new(localCache.Root.Position, root_.Position), root_)
				elseif tbl2.bang then
					bangTime = bangTime + deltaTime
					fn8(root_.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3.5 + math.sin(bangTime * bangSpeed * 10) * 1.8), root_)
				elseif tbl2.faceBang and head then
					bangTime = bangTime + deltaTime
					local n = math.sin(bangTime * bangSpeed * 10) * 1.8
					fn8(head.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, 3.1415926535897931, 0) * CFrame.new(0, 1, 2.5 + n), head)
				elseif tbl2.tweenPlayer then
					if currentTween then
						currentTween:Cancel()
						currentTween = nil
					end

					local magnitude = (localCache.Root.Position - root_.Position).Magnitude

					if magnitude > 0.5 then
						local tbl3 = { CFrame = root_.CFrame }
						currentTween = TweenService:Create(localCache.Root, TweenInfo.new(magnitude / tweenSpeed, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), tbl3)
						currentTween:Play()
					end
				elseif tbl2.headSit and head then
					fn8(head.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0), head)
				elseif tbl2.backpack then
					fn8(root_.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 1) * CFrame.Angles(0, 3.1415926535897931, 0), root_)
				elseif tbl2.carpet then
					fn8(root_.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -3.6, 0) * CFrame.Angles(1.5707963267948966, 0, 0), root_)
				end

				if tbl2.walkTo then
					if tbl2.smartWalk then
						pathCalcDebounce = pathCalcDebounce + deltaTime

						if (root_.Position - lastTargetPos).Magnitude > 5 and pathCalcDebounce > 0.5 then
							pathCalcDebounce = 0
							lastTargetPos = root_.Position

							task.spawn(function()
								-- proto t845
								local v = PathfindingService:CreatePath({ AgentRadius = 2, AgentHeight = 5, AgentCanJump = true, WaypointSpacing = 4 })
								v:ComputeAsync(localCache.Root.Position, root_.Position)

								if v.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then
									walkPath = v:GetWaypoints()
									walkIndex = 2
								end
							end)
						end

						if walkPath and walkPath[walkIndex] then
							local position = walkPath[walkIndex].Position

							if (localCache.Root.Position * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1) - position * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)).Magnitude < 3 then
								walkIndex = walkIndex + 1
							end

							localCache.Humanoid:MoveTo(position)
						end
					else
						localCache.Humanoid:MoveTo(root_.Position)
					end
				end

				if tbl2.fling then
					flingTimer = flingTimer + deltaTime

					if flingTimer > 0.5 and not isFlinging then
						flingTimer = 0
						fn9()
					end
				end
			end)

			local function fn12()
				-- proto t834
				for k in pairs(tbl2) do
					tbl2[k] = false
				end

				espEnabled = false
				camera.CameraSubject = localCache.Humanoid or originalCameraSubject
				fn5()

				if currentTween then
					currentTween:Cancel()
					currentTween = nil
				end

				if antiGravityVelocity then
					antiGravityVelocity:Destroy()
					antiGravityVelocity = nil
				end

				if localCache.Character then
					for _, descendant in ipairs(localCache.Character:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
							descendant.CanCollide = true
						end
					end
				end

				if localCache.Humanoid then
					localCache.Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
				end

				if headSitSaved then
					fn8(headSitSaved)

					if localCache.Humanoid then
						localCache.Humanoid.Sit = false
					end

					headSitSaved = nil
				end

				if backpackSaved then
					fn8(backpackSaved)

					if localCache.Humanoid then
						localCache.Humanoid.Sit = false
					end

					backpackSaved = nil
				end
			end

			local function fn13(character_)
				-- proto t835
				task.spawn(function()
					-- proto t837
					fn3(localCache, character_)

					if localCache.Root then
						tbl.OldPos = localCache.Root.CFrame
					end

					if localCache.Humanoid then
						camera.CameraSubject = localCache.Humanoid
					end

					if toolToggleEnabled then
						task.delay(1, fn11)
					end
				end)
			end

					-- proto t1016

				for _, player_ in ipairs(Players_:GetPlayers()) do

							fn11(player_, player_.Character)

	AntiBanTab = function()
		-- proto t1944
		task.spawn(function()
			-- proto t10673
			Vars = { AnticheatConn = nil }
			Options = { AvoidBanMethod = { Value = "Kick" }, RejoinDelay = { Value = "3" } }

			Rejoin = function(arg)
				-- proto t1060
				task.wait(arg or 3)

				if #Players:GetPlayers() <= 1 then
					Players.LocalPlayer:Kick("Rejoining...")
					task.wait()
					TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer)
				else
					TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer)
				end
			end

			HandleSafetyAction = function(arg)
				-- proto t1061
				Notify("Anticheat Flagged", arg)
				local value = Options.AvoidBanMethod.Value

				if value == "Auto Leave" or value == "Kick" then
					LocalPlayer:Kick("[Anticheat] Safety Kick: " .. arg)
				elseif value == "Auto Rejoin" then
					Rejoin(tonumber(Options.RejoinDelay.Value))
				elseif value == "Warn" then
					warn("[Anticheat] Flagged: " .. arg)
				end
			end

			AntibanSex = Tabs.AntiBan:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Antiban ", Icon = "shield-alert" })

			AntibanSex:Dropdown({
				Title = "Safety Action ",
				Flag = "tsbsafelyactionantiban",
				Values = { "None", "Warn", "Auto Leave", "Auto Rejoin", "Kick" },
				Value = "Kick",
				Locked = LockedStatus,
				LockedTitle = "Premium",
				Callback = function(value)
					-- proto t1059
					Options.AvoidBanMethod.Value = value
				end,
			})

			AntibanSex:Input({
				Title = "Rejoin Delay ",
				Flag = "tsbrejoindaleyantiban",
				Locked = LockedStatus,
				LockedTitle = "Premium",
				Value = "3",
				Numeric = true,
				Finished = true,
				Callback = function(value)
					-- proto t1058
					Options.RejoinDelay.Value = value
				end,
			})

			AntibanSex:Toggle({
				Title = "Enable Antiban Detector ",
				Flag = "tsbantibanmaintoggle",
				Locked = LockedStatus,
				LockedTitle = "Premium",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t1062
					if arg then
						if not Vars.AnticheatConn then
							local replication = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Replication")

							if replication then
								Vars.AnticheatConn = replication.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
									-- proto t1063
									local v = ({ ... })[1]

									if type(v) == "table" then
										local value = rawget(v, "Effect")

										if value then
											value = tostring(value):lower() == "hicheck"
										end

										if value then
											HandleSafetyAction("A1 (Report/HiCheck)")
										end
									end
								end)
							end
						end
					elseif Vars.AnticheatConn then
						Vars.AnticheatConn:Disconnect()
						Vars.AnticheatConn = nil
					end
				end,
			})

			Antistaff = Tabs.AntiBan:Section({ Opened = true, Box = true, BoxBorder = true, Title = "Anti Staff", Icon = "shield" })
			Players = game:GetService("Players")
			ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
			LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
			TargetGroupId = 12013007

			BlacklistedUserIds = {
				422755031,
				198131804,
				681405668,
				3414432341,
				339633571,
				430966809,
				2039323684,
				117723419,
				1015595932,
				263944298,
				112905203,
				2284964418,
				1266437961,
				3120648134,
				1148139861,
				1633233654,
				3350014406,
				971193650,
				661273560,
				66105529,
				77342385,
				167343092,
				2055306963,
				141984224,
				438917845,
				1391134999,
				1796550069,
				255671730,
				3162123826,
				1059541187,
				1259898795,
				31070091,
				1041867508,
				994994173,
				1446694201,
				77525605,
				1001242712,
				2533866869,
				4983064295,
			}

			activeChecks = { Moderators = true, Contributor = true, Developer = true, Owner = true, Other = true }
			isEnabled = false
			connection = nil

			KickForStaffPresence = function(staff)
				-- proto t1064
				LocalPlayer:Kick("Staff: " .. staff)
			end

			CheckPlayer = function(arg)
				-- proto t1065
				if arg == LocalPlayer or not isEnabled then
					return
				end

				task.spawn(function()
					-- proto t1066
					if arg:IsInGroup(TargetGroupId) then
						role = arg:GetRoleInGroup(TargetGroupId)

						if activeChecks.Moderators and role:find("Moderator") then
							KickForStaffPresence("Mod")
						end

						if activeChecks.Contributor and role:find("Contributor") then
							KickForStaffPresence("Contrib")
						end

						if activeChecks.Developer and role:find("Developer") then
							KickForStaffPresence("Dev")
						end

						if activeChecks.Owner and role:find("Owner") then
							KickForStaffPresence("Owner")
						end
					end

					if activeChecks["Others..."] then
						for _, v in ipairs(BlacklistedUserIds) do
							if arg.UserId == v then
								KickForStaffPresence("ID " .. v)
							end

							if arg:IsFriendsWith(v) then
								KickForStaffPresence("Friend " .. v)
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			Antistaff:Dropdown({
				Title = "Target Roles",
				Flag = "tsbtargetrolesantistaff",
				Multi = true,
				Values = { "Moderators", "Contributor", "Developer", "Owner", "Other" },
				Options = { "Moderators", "Contributor", "Developer", "Owner", "Other" },
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t1055
					for k in pairs(activeChecks) do
						activeChecks[k] = false
					end

					for _, v in ipairs(arg) do
						activeChecks[v] = true
					end
				end,
			})

			Antistaff:Toggle({
				Title = "Enable Detection",
				Flag = "tsbantistaffmaintoggle",
				Value = false,
				Callback = function(arg)
					-- proto t1056
					isEnabled = arg

					if isEnabled then
						for _, player_ in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
							CheckPlayer(player_)
						end

						connection = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(CheckPlayer)
					elseif connection then
						connection:Disconnect()
						connection = nil
					end
				end,
			})

			if ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Replication") then
				ReplicatedStorage.Replication.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(arg)
					-- proto t1057
					if isEnabled and arg and arg.Effect and arg.Effect:lower() == "hicheck" then
						LocalPlayer:Kick("A1 detected")
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
	end

			end

			local function fn11(arg, arg2)
				-- proto t1152
				if arg2.Magnitude < 1e-06 then
					return nil
				end
				fn9()

				if not hit then
					return nil

					if hit2 then

				end

			end

			local function fn12(arg, arg2, arg3)
				-- proto t1153
				if not obj4[arg] then
					obj4[arg] = { StartCF = arg.CFrame, LastCF = arg.CFrame, PredictedPos = nil, AnimId = arg2 }
				end

				local v2 = obj4[arg]

				if v2.AnimId ~= arg2 then
					obj4[arg] = { StartCF = arg.CFrame, LastCF = arg.CFrame, PredictedPos = nil, AnimId = arg2 }
					v2 = obj4[arg]
				end

				local position = arg.Position

				if v2.PredictedPos then
					if (position - v2.PredictedPos).Magnitude < 10 then
						v2.PredictedPos = nil
						return nil
					end
					return v2.PredictedPos
				end

				local v3 = v2.StartCF:PointToObjectSpace(position)
				local n2 = position - v2.StartCF.Position
				local flag2, unit

				if arg3.Name == "Doom Dive" or arg3.Name == "Doom Dive Alt" then
					local flag3 = (v2.LastCF.Position.Y - position.Y > 0.4 or v2.StartCF.Position.Y - position.Y > 2.5) and n2.Magnitude > 0.05
					flag2 = false
					unit = nil

					if flag3 then
						unit = (position - v2.LastCF.Position).Unit
						flag2 = true
					end
				elseif arg3.RaycastType == "DownOnly" then
					flag2 = false
					unit = nil

					if v3.Y < -2 then
						flag2 = true
						unit = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0)
					end
				else
					flag2 = false
					unit = nil

					if arg3.RaycastType == "ForwardDown" then
						local flag3 = v3.Z < -3 and v3.Y < -0.5 and n2.Magnitude > 0.05
						unit = nil

						if flag3 then
							unit = n2.Unit
							flag2 = true
						end
					end
				end

				v2.LastCF = arg.CFrame

				if flag2 and unit then
					local v4 = fn11(position, unit)

					if not v4 and arg3.Name:find("Doom Dive") then
						v4 = fn11(position, Vector3.new(0, -1, 0))
					end

					if v4 then
						v2.PredictedPos = v4
						return v4
					end
				end

				return nil
			end

			local function fn15(arg)
				-- proto t1159
				local v2 = fn14()
				if not v2 then
					return nil, nil
				end
				local position = v2.Position
				local n2 = arg * arg
				local v3 = n
				local huge = math.huge
				local v4 = nil

				for k in pairs(tbl4) do
					if k and k.Parent then
						if not k:FindFirstChild("M1ing") then
							tbl4[k] = nil
						end

						local head = k:FindFirstChild("Head")
						local humanoidRootPart_ = k:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if head and humanoidRootPart_ then
							local assemblyLinearVelocity = humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
							local magnitude = assemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude

							if magnitude > 200 then
								assemblyLinearVelocity *= 200 / magnitude
							end

							local n3 = head.Position + assemblyLinearVelocity * v3 - position
							local n4 = n3.X * n3.X + n3.Y * n3.Y + n3.Z * n3.Z
							local flag2

							if n4 <= 49 then
								flag2 = true
							else
								flag2 = false

								if n4 <= n2 then
									if n4 > 0.01 then
										if humanoidRootPart_.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(-n3.Unit) > 0.422 then
											flag2 = true
										end
									else
										flag2 = true
									end
								end
							end

							flag2 = flag2 and n4 < huge

							if flag2 then
								huge = n4
								v4 = k
							end
						end
					else
						tbl4[k] = nil
					end
				end

				return v4, huge ~= math.huge and huge or nil
			end

			local function fn16(arg, arg2)
				-- proto t1137
				local n2 = 1

				pcall(function()
					-- proto t1139
					n2 = math.max(arg.Speed, 0.01)
				end)

				local n3 = arg.TimePosition * n2

				if arg2.IgnoreAfter and n3 > arg2.IgnoreAfter then

				return true
			end

			local function fn17(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5, arg6, arg7)
				-- proto t1140
				local humanoidRootPart_ = arg and arg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not humanoidRootPart_ then
					return false
				end

				if not arg5 then
					arg5 = fn4(arg2.Animation and arg2.Animation.AnimationId)
				end

				arg4 = arg4 or arg5 and _G.CounterData[arg5] or nil
				if not arg4 then
					return false
				end
				local v2 = arg7 or fn13(arg4.Name)

				if arg7 and not arg6 then
					local v3 = tbl[arg7]
					if v3 and not v3[arg4.Name] then
						return false
					end
				end

				if not v2 and not arg6 then
					return false
				end
				local n2 = 1

				pcall(function()
					-- proto t1142
					n2 = math.max(arg2.Speed, 0.01)
				end)

				local n3 = arg2.TimePosition * n2
				if arg4.Duration and n3 > arg4.Duration then
					return false
				end

				if arg4.Delay and n3 < arg4.Delay then
					return false
				end

				if arg4.IgnoreAfter and n3 > arg4.IgnoreAfter then
					return false
				end
				local assemblyLinearVelocity

				if arg4.PosPredict then
					local position = humanoidRootPart_.Position
					local now = tick()
					local v3 = obj3[humanoidRootPart_]

					if not v3 then
						obj3[humanoidRootPart_] = { LastPos = position, LastTime = now }
						assemblyLinearVelocity = humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
					else
						local n4 = now - v3.LastTime

						if n4 > 0 then
							assemblyLinearVelocity = (position - v3.LastPos) / n4
						else
							assemblyLinearVelocity = humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
						end

						v3.LastPos = position
						v3.LastTime = now
					end
				else
					assemblyLinearVelocity = humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
				end

				local magnitude = (humanoidRootPart_.Position + assemblyLinearVelocity * (arg4.PosPredict or arg4.Predict or 0) - arg3.Position).Magnitude

				if arg4.RaycastType then
					local v3 = fn12(humanoidRootPart_, arg5, arg4)

					if v3 then
						if (v3 - arg3.Position).Magnitude <= arg4.Dist then
							return true
						end
					end

					if magnitude <= (arg4.Dist or 20) then
						return true
					end
					return false
				end

				if arg4.Special == "StoneCoffin" then
					local n4 = humanoidRootPart_.Position + humanoidRootPart_.CFrame.LookVector * 19.5 + Vector3.new(0, 20, 0)
					local raycastParams2 = RaycastParams.new()
					raycastParams2.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
					raycastParams2.FilterDescendantsInstances = { localPlayer_.Character, arg }
					local hit = workspace:Raycast(n4, Vector3.new(0, -1000, 0), raycastParams2)
					if not hit then
						return false
					end
					return (hit.Position - arg3.Position).Magnitude <= 10.5
				end

				if arg4.Special == "BoxCheck" then
					local n4 = humanoidRootPart_.Position + (humanoidRootPart_.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero) * (arg4.Predict or 0)
					local rotation = humanoidRootPart_.CFrame.Rotation
					local v3 = (CFrame.new(n4) * rotation):PointToObjectSpace(arg3.Position)
					if v3.Z >= 0 then
						return false
					end

					if v3.Z < -arg4.OffsetZ then
						return false
					end

					if arg4.Width < math.abs(v3.X) then
						return false
					end

					if (arg4.Height or 20) < math.abs(v3.Y) then
						return false
					end

					for _, child in ipairs(auraTemplate:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("BasePart") then
							charPart = arg:FindFirstChild(child.Name)

							if charPart then
								for _, child2 in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
									if child2:IsA("Attachment") or child2:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
										clone = child2:Clone()
										clone:SetAttribute("LimitedAura", true)
										clone.Parent = charPart

										task.delay(60, function()
											-- proto t1224
											TweenService:Create(sound, TweenInfo.new(0.5), { Volume = 0 }):Play()

											task.delay(0.75, function()
												-- proto t1225
												if sound and sound.Parent then
													sound:Destroy()
												end
											end)

											if clone:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
												clone.Enabled = false
											else
												for _, child3 in ipairs(clone:GetChildren()) do
													if child3:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or child3:IsA("Beam") then
														child3.Enabled = false
													end
												end
											end
										end)

										task.delay(65, function()
											-- proto t1223
											if clone and clone.Parent then
												clone:Destroy()
											end
										end)
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end

					auraTemplate:Destroy()
				end,
			}

			InnerRageCallback = function()
				-- proto t1216
				character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
				humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
				local random2 = math.random
				baseColor = Color3.fromRGB(math.random(100, 255), math.random(50, 150), random2(50, 150))
				local random3 = math.random
				randColor = Color3.fromRGB(math.random(0, 255), math.random(0, 255), random3(0, 255))
				local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
				animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://96993907314948"
				local v = humanoid:LoadAnimation(animation)
				v:Play()

				v.Stopped:Connect(function()
					-- proto t1221
					local animation2 = Instance.new("Animation")
					animation2.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://127234845846317"
					humanoid:LoadAnimation(animation2):Play()
				end)

				acc = Instance.new("Accessory")
				acc.Name = "#EmoteHolder_" .. math.random(1, 100000)
				acc.Parent = character
				CollectionService:AddTag(acc, "emoteendstuff" .. character.Name)

				require(ReplicatedStorage.Emotes.VFX):MainFunction({
					Character = character,
					vfxName = "Energy Explosion",
					AnimSent = 96993907314948,
					RealBind = acc,
					NoInsertion = true,
					Colour = randColor,
				})

				fxList = {}

				task.delay(1.3, function()
					-- proto t1217
					if not acc.Parent then
						return
					end
					hairHandles = {}

					for _, child in ipairs(character.FakeHead:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("Accessory") and child:FindFirstChild("Handle") and child.Handle:FindFirstChild("HairAttachment") then
							handle = child.Handle
							table.insert(hairHandles, handle)

							for _, child2 in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
								if child2:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
									child2:SetAttribute("basetext", child2.TextureId)
								end
							end
						end
					end

					for _, v2 in ipairs(hairHandles) do
						clone = v2:Clone()
						weld = Instance.new("Weld")
						weld.Part0 = clone
						weld.Part1 = v2
						weld.Parent = clone
						clone.Parent = workspace.Thrown
						v2.Transparency = 1
						TweenService:Create(v2, TweenInfo.new(0.25), { Transparency = 0 }):Play()
						mesh = v2:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpecialMesh")

						if mesh then
							mesh.TextureId = ""
							glow = ReplicatedStorage.Resources.DeathEffect.Template:Clone()
							glow.Color3 = Color3.new(baseColor.R * 5, baseColor.G * 5, baseColor.B * 5)
							glow.Parent = clone
						end
					end

					auraHolder = Instance.new("Folder")
					auraHolder.Name = "AuraHolder"
					auraHolder:SetAttribute("LimAura", true)
					auraHolder:SetAttribute("EmoteEffect", true)
					auraHolder.Parent = character

					task.delay(203, function()
						-- proto t1219
						if auraHolder then
							auraHolder:Destroy()
						end
					end)

					for _, child in ipairs(ReplicatedStorage.Emotes.AuraReal:GetChildren()) do
						clone = child:Clone()
						clone:SetAttribute("LimAura", true)

						if clone:IsA("Attachment") then
							clone.Parent = character.PrimaryPart
						else
							weld = Instance.new("Weld")
							weld.Part0 = character.PrimaryPart
							weld.Part1 = clone
							weld.Parent = clone
						end

						for _, descendant in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
							if descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant:IsA("PointLight") then
								descendant.Enabled = false
								descendant:SetAttribute("LimitedAura", true)
								descendant:SetAttribute("InnerRageAura", true)

								if descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
									descendant.Color = ColorSequence.new(baseColor)
								end

								if descendant:IsA("PointLight") then
									descendant.Color = baseColor
									descendant.Brightness = 1.3
								end

								table.insert(fxList, descendant)
							end
						end

						task.delay(203, function()
							-- proto t1218
							if clone then
								clone:Destroy()
							end
						end)
					end
				end)

				task.delay(5.3, function()
					-- proto t1220
					if not acc.Parent then
						return
					end

					for _, v2 in ipairs(fxList) do
						v2.Enabled = true
					end

					task.wait(0.05)

					for _, v2 in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
						if v2.Animation.AnimationId == "rbxassetid://127234845846317" then
							v2:Stop()
							finalAnim = Instance.new("Animation")
							finalAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://117177504280717"
							humanoid:LoadAnimation(finalAnim):Play()
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			for _, v in ipairs(tbl2) do
				CSLimitedEmotesex:Button({
					Title = v.Title,
					Icon = "mouse-pointer-click",
					IconAlign = "Right",
					Locked = false,
					LockedTitle = "Locked",
					Justify = "Between",
					Callback = v._useDirectCallback and InnerRageCallback or function()
						-- proto t1215
						fn(v)
					end,
				})
			end
		end)
	end

	end

	LoadVexonHub()
end

executorResult = checkExecutorSupport()
StartNormalVersion()
